# Backward curation manifest: linuxserver_research

Source: `/Users/user/CODE/linuxserver_research` (read-only). Destination payload prefix: `linuxserver_research/`.

**Traversal:** files and directories were enumerated and processed in descending alphabetical path order. The full source had 5,233 files. Before copying, 256 selected text/structured research files (5,426,296 bytes) were scanned for credential patterns; no plausible secret was detected. Copied bytes were SHA-256 verified against source after copy.

## LEDGER.md / PLAN.md full-read connection to the Coin_Trade log

**This is additional, directly relevant KR/US systematic-strategy trial history and materially narrows the missing-history gap, but it does not establish a single deduplicated global cumulative N.** The independent source program began 2026-11-13 and targeted short-term KR/US stock strategies; Coin_Trade's related Gen14–Gen18 log is from the separate Ochestration workspace and explicitly says “Global cumulative N is unknown.” The subject matter overlaps (for example, short-horizon reversal, momentum, trend, and calendar families), and the counting units differ: the source ledger counts named KR ideas and US hypothesis/universe variants plus tuning cells; the Coin_Trade log includes frozen candidates, implementation variants, and later generations whose full Oracle trial database is unavailable. Do not add these figures and call the sum a complete global N without candidate-level deduplication and a common trial definition.

Concrete source-ledger evidence:

- **KR:** LEDGER contains KR-01 through KR-36: 12 initial S1 ideas, KR-13–KR-24 (explicitly “Total trials 24” after round 2), and KR-25–KR-36. The listed outcomes are `noise` or `hurts`; there is **no KR advance/pass** in these 36. Named examples include “KR-24 post-crash rebound with volume dry-up” (net +1.422%, t=2.29, verdict `noise`, below the stated S1 threshold) and “KR-35 monthly momentum with KOSPI index trend filter” (net -3.424%, t=-2.08, `noise`).
- **US:** The ledger gives a cumulative count of **207 trials** by US round 9: 30 (round 1), 15 more (cum 45), 54 Stage-2 grid cells (cum 99), 13 (cum 112), 17 (cum 129), 11 (cum 140), 9 survivorship-repair trials (cum 149), 47 (cum 196), and 11 (cum 207). It explicitly records **0 survivors** in round 1 (0/30), round 4 (0/13), round 8 (0/21 spec-by-hold groups under its stricter gate), and round 9 (0); final strict gate says **0 survivorship-robust candidates**. The one explicitly named technical Stage-3 pass is `US-18-T100` / `US-18-T100-cand`: “momentum-leader dip” (RSI2<5, 126d top quartile, exit above SMA5 or 5d), with 410 holdout trades, net +1.98%/trade, t=3.31, N=10 Sharpe 2.01, 2x cost +1.58%, satisfying six technical criteria. The same ledger immediately caveats strong survivorship/regime dependence; frozen-universe holdout metrics fall to +0.99% (2024 freeze) and +0.45% (2014 freeze), and concludes “0 survivorship-robust candidates.”
- **PLAN:** Sets an objective of several short-term KR/US strategies, final 24-month holdout, preregistered screens, capped parameter tuning, multiple-testing correction and a Stage-3 gate; it sets six rounds per market. The ledger went beyond that six-round cap for the US after an owner instruction and completed nine US rounds, so the 207 total includes that explicitly authorized extra research.

Coin_Trade's separate log records Gen14 **0/6 candidate-market combinations and 0/12 candidate-cost cells passed**, with current-survivor input caveats. Gen15 was data-blocked before performance; Gen16/Gen17 exploratory outcomes were not published in that log; Gen18 has no performance preregistration. It says the Oracle cumulative trial databases are unavailable and global cumulative N unknown. The stock_loop ledger adds a concrete, named history—**36 KR ideas plus 207 US trials (243 ledger-counted units across the two markets, not a deduplicated global total)**—and surfaces the `US-18-T100` technical pass alongside its survivorship caveat. It cannot resolve all of Coin_Trade's global-N uncertainty because overlapping candidates and counting conventions are not reconciled. Treat 243 as source-ledger evidence only, not the corrected combined trial count.
